      "source": [
        "# Intelligent Health Facility Recommendation System"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "zIvwt75ZUtcy"
      },
      "source": [
        "## Introduction\n",
        "\n",
        "This notebook builds the recommendation engine for the health facility recommendation system, using the cleaned datasets from the data preparation stage.\n",
        "\n",
        "The engine turns a user's healthcare need into a ranked list of facilities, based on clinical relevance, service availability, facility capability, and distance rather than a plain keyword search. Each candidate facility gets a weighted score so results can be ranked by fit.\n",
        "\n",
        "The approach starts rule-based and is extended toward a machine-learning-based matching stage further in the notebook."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "YG5EUtxJWwJ5"
      },
      "source": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "3e9bda49"
      },
      "source": [
        "### Recommendation workflow\n",
        "\n",
        "1. User enters a healthcare need in natural language.\n",
        "2. The query is matched to the closest health need in the taxonomy.\n",
        "3. The matched need is translated into its standardized service (`Standardized_Services`), since `Service_Group` is too broad a category for retrieval.\n",
        "4. Facilities offering that service, at or above the required KEPH level, are pulled from the Master Facility List.\n",
        "5. Candidates are scored on service match, facility level, and distance.\n",
        "6. Scores are combined into one ranking, with emergency needs boosted.\n",
        "7. Each recommendation comes with a short explanation.\n",
        "8. The pipeline is tested end-to-end against sample queries."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "_56yLofDW-KU"
      },
      "source": [

      ]

        "## Parse structured fields\n",
        "\n",
        "Some of fields need converting before they're usable:\n",
        "\n",
        "- `Services` is stored as a stringified list, so it's parsed with `ast.literal_eval` into an actual Python list for matching/counting.\n",
        "- `Keph Level` and `Minimum_KEPH_Level` are text values; the numeric level is pulled out so facility capability can be compared against what a health need requires."
      ]
    },

      "source": [
        "# Parse services list and keph levels\n",
        "\n",
        "def parse_services_list(raw):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Converts the stringified list in the Services column into an actual\n",
        "    Python list of service names.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    if pd.isna(raw):\n",
        "        return []\n",
        "    if isinstance(raw, list):\n",
        "        return raw\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        parsed = ast.literal_eval(raw)\n",
        "        if isinstance(parsed, list):\n",
        "            return [str(s).strip() for s in parsed if str(s).strip()]\n",
        "    except (ValueError, SyntaxError):\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "    # Fallback for malformed entries: strip brackets/quotes and split on comma\n",
        "    cleaned = str(raw).strip(\"[]\")\n",
        "    items = [item.strip(\" '\\\"\") for item in cleaned.split(\",\")]\n",
        "    return [i for i in items if i]\n",
        "\n",
        "def parse_keph_level(value):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Extracts the numeric KEPH level from strings like \"Level 3\" -> 3.\n",
        "    Returns None if no digit is found.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    if pd.isna(value):\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    match = re.search(r\"(\\d+)\", str(value))\n",
        "    return int(match.group(1)) if match else None\n",
        "\n",
        "master_facilities[\"Services_Parsed\"] = master_facilities[\"Services\"].apply(\n",
        "    parse_services_list\n",
        ")\n",
        "master_facilities[\"Keph_Level_Numeric\"] = master_facilities[\"Keph Level\"].apply(\n",
        "    parse_keph_level\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "taxonomy[\"Minimum_KEPH_Level_Numeric\"] = taxonomy[\"Minimum_KEPH_Level\"].apply(\n",
        "    parse_keph_level\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"Parsed\",\n",
        "    master_facilities[\"Services_Parsed\"].apply(len).gt(0).sum(),\n",
        "    \"of\",\n",
        "    len(master_facilities),\n",
        "    \"facilities with at least one listed service\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "display(\n",
        "    master_facilities[\n",
        "        [\"Facility Name\", \"Keph Level\", \"Keph_Level_Numeric\", \"Services_Parsed\"]\n",
        "    ].head()\n",
        ")\n",
        "display(\n",
        "    taxonomy[\n",
        "        [\n",
        "            \"User_Health_Need\",\n",
        "            \"Service_Group\",\n",
        "            \"Standardized_Services\",\n",
        "            \"Minimum_KEPH_Level\",\n",
        "            \"Minimum_KEPH_Level_Numeric\",\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    ].head()\n",
        ")\n"
      ]

      "source": [
        "# Query understanding\n",
        "\n",
        "Users describe their concern in their own words, so the first step is mapping that free text to the closest health need in the taxonomy.\n",
        "\n",
        "A search index is built from each taxonomy entry (health need, keywords, symptoms, example queries) to give the matcher more to work with. The output is the best-matching health need and its standardized service, used to retrieve facilities in the next stages."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "0N0IrrLuniyj"
      },
      "source": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "892b6623"
      },
      "source": [
        "## Text preprocessing\n",
        "\n",
        "The query is lowercased, stripped of punctuation, and cleaned of extra whitespace before it's compared against the taxonomy. This keeps small wording differences from throwing off the match."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "617577ce"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "# Text preprocessing\n",
        "\n",
        "def preprocess_text(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Standardize text for matching.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    # Handle missing values\n",
        "    if pd.isna(text):\n",
        "        return \"\"\n",
        "    # Convert to string and lowercase\n",
        "    text = str(text).lower()\n",
        "    # Remove punctuation\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"[^\\w\\s]\", \"\", text)\n",
        "    # Remove extra whitespace\n",
        "    text = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", text).strip()\n",
        "    return text\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 224
        },
        "id": "7acfa15c",
        "outputId": "bee0519c-9bc4-4076-dbff-2d91a255555a"
      },

        "# Create taxonomy search index\n",
        "\n",
        "taxonomy[\"Search_Index\"] = (\n",
        "    taxonomy[\"User_Health_Need\"].fillna(\"\")\n",
        "    + \" \"\n",
        "    + taxonomy[\"Search_Keywords\"].fillna(\"\")\n",
        "    + \" \"\n",
        "    + taxonomy[\"Common_Symptoms\"].fillna(\"\")\n",
        "    + \" \"\n",
        "    + taxonomy[\"Example_User_Queries\"].fillna(\"\")\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Apply preprocessing\n",
        "taxonomy[\"Search_Index\"] = taxonomy[\"Search_Index\"].apply(preprocess_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Taxonomy search index created successfully\")\n",
        "\n",
        "display(taxonomy[[\"User_Health_Need\", \"Search_Index\"]].head())\n"
      ]

        "id": "SNsz8wZy0hAS"

      "source": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "a34c77f5"
      },
      "source": [
        "## Match user query to health need\n",
        "\n",
        "Match the query against the taxonomy — not to diagnose, just to find the closest predefined need.\n",
        "\n",
        "Three signals are combined:\n",
        "\n",
        "1. Health need similarity — query vs. the taxonomy's search index.\n",
        "2. Service similarity — query vs. the standardized service.\n",
        "3. Clinical signal score — keyword check for terms like \"chest pain\", \"hiv\", \"accident\", since fuzzy matching alone can miss short but important phrases.\n",
        "\n",
        "Emergency-related needs get an extra boost so urgent cases aren't outranked by better text matches on routine needs. The top-ranked need is passed on to facility retrieval."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "cdf15976"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "clinical_signals = {\n",
        "    \"emergency\": [\n",
        "        \"accident\",\n",
        "        \"severe injury\",\n",
        "        \"unconscious\",\n",
        "        \"not responding\",\n",
        "        \"severe bleeding\",\n",
        "        \"chest pain\",\n",
        "        \"heart attack\",\n",
        "        \"burn\",\n",
        "    ],\n",
        "    \"hiv\": [\"hiv\", \"aids\", \"antiretroviral\", \"art\"],\n",
        "    \"child_health\": [\"child\", \"baby\", \"infant\", \"fever\"],\n",
        "    \"malaria\": [\"malaria\", \"fever\", \"chills\"],\n",
        "    \"family_planning\": [\"family planning\", \"contraceptive\", \"implant\", \"iud\"],\n",
        "}\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "fdd7e386"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def detect_clinical_signals(query, signal_dictionary):\n",
        "\n",
        "    query = query.lower()\n",
        "    detected = []\n",
        "    for signal_group, keywords in signal_dictionary.items():\n",
        "        for keyword in keywords:\n",
        "            if keyword in query:\n",
        "                detected.append(signal_group)\n",
        "                break\n",
        "    return detected\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "0284180c"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def calculate_clinical_signal_score(row, detected_signals):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Scores a taxonomy row by checking whether it belongs to the same\n",
        "    clinical signal group(s) as the user's query, using the same keyword\n",
        "    dictionary on both sides for a consistent, symmetric comparison.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    row_text = (\n",
        "        str(row[\"User_Health_Need\"])\n",
        "        + \" \"\n",
        "        + str(row[\"Parent_Category\"])\n",
        "        + \" \"\n",
        "        + str(row[\"Service_Group\"])\n",
        "        + \" \"\n",
        "        + str(row[\"Standardized_Services\"])\n",
        "        + \" \"\n",
        "        + str(row.get(\"Search_Keywords\", \"\"))\n",
        "        + \" \"\n",
        "        + str(row.get(\"Common_Symptoms\", \"\"))\n",
        "    ).lower()\n",
        "    row_signals = set(detect_clinical_signals(row_text, clinical_signals))\n",
        "    overlap = row_signals.intersection(detected_signals)\n",
        "    return 100 if overlap else 0\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "347a83dd"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def match_health_needs(query, taxonomy_df):\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = taxonomy_df.copy()\n",
        "    query = preprocess_text(query)\n",
        "    results[\"Health_Need_Score\"] = results[\"Search_Index\"].apply(\n",
        "        lambda x: fuzz.token_set_ratio(query, str(x))\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    results[\"Service_Score\"] = results[\"Standardized_Services\"].apply(\n",
        "        lambda x: fuzz.token_set_ratio(query, str(x).lower())\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return results\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "e7b06e8f"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def apply_clinical_scores(results, query):\n",
        "\n",
        "    signals = detect_clinical_signals(query, clinical_signals)\n",
        "    results[\"Clinical_Signal_Score\"] = results.apply(\n",
        "        lambda row: calculate_clinical_signal_score(row, signals), axis=1\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return results\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "e56677db"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def apply_emergency_boost(results):\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = results.copy()\n",
        "    results.loc[results[\"Emergency_Related\"] == \"Yes\", \"Final_Match_Score\"] += 15\n",
        "    return results\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "39939c9d"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def calculate_final_score(df):\n",
        "\n",
        "    df[\"Final_Match_Score\"] = (\n",
        "        df[\"Health_Need_Score\"] * 0.45\n",
        "        + df[\"Service_Score\"] * 0.25\n",
        "        + df[\"Clinical_Signal_Score\"] * 0.30\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return df\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "c5fc75a3"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def recommend_health_needs(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy, top_n=5):\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = match_health_needs(query, taxonomy_df)\n",
        "    results = apply_clinical_scores(results, query)\n",
        "    results = calculate_final_score(results)\n",
        "    results = apply_emergency_boost(results)\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\"Final_Match_Score\", ascending=False)\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)\n"
      ]
    },

              "                                                    [key], {});\n",
              "        if (!dataTable) return;\n",
              "\n",
              "        const docLinkHtml = 'Like what you see? Visit the ' +\n",
              "          '<a target=\"_blank\" href=https://colab.research.google.com/notebooks/data_table.ipynb>data table notebook</a>'\n",

              "        element.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "        dataTable['output_type'] = 'display_data';\n",

              "        const docLink = document.createElement('div');\n",

        "            ]\n",

      "metadata": {
        "id": "PhukRp0U8X8D"

      "source": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "bd9e887a"
      },
      "source": [
        "# Smarter query matching with TF-IDF and cosine similarity\n",
        "\n",
        "Fuzzy matching (rapidfuzz) compares strings character by character, so it has no real notion of meaning — it won't connect \"my chest feels tight and I can't breathe\" to \"chest pain\".\n",
        "\n",
        "TF-IDF turns each taxonomy entry into a vector, weighting distinctive clinical words (\"antiretroviral\", \"contraceptive\") more than common ones. Cosine similarity then compares the angle between the query vector and each taxonomy vector, so differently worded text can still score well if the distinctive vocabulary overlaps.\n",
        "\n",
        "This is a standard technique, trains in milliseconds here, and unlike sentence embeddings needs no model download. The clinical-signal keyword boost from the previous section stays as-is."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "39e0f889",
        "outputId": "f576180c-4b0b-47ee-904c-271f2c9749ae"
      },
      "outputs": [
        {
          "name": "stdout",
          "output_type": "stream",
          "text": [
            "Health-need vocabulary size: 7088\n",
            "Service vocabulary size: 613\n"
          ]
        }
      ],
      "source": [
        "from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n",
        "\n",
        "# One TF-IDF space over the rich, multi-field Search_Index (health need matching)\n",
        "need_vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n",
        "    ngram_range=(1, 2), min_df=1, sublinear_tf=True, stop_words=\"english\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "need_tfidf_matrix = need_vectorizer.fit_transform(taxonomy[\"Search_Index\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "# A second TF-IDF space over just the standardized service names (service matching)\n",
        "service_vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(\n",
        "    ngram_range=(1, 2), min_df=1, sublinear_tf=True, stop_words=\"english\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "service_tfidf_matrix = service_vectorizer.fit_transform(\n",
        "    taxonomy[\"Standardized_Services\"].fillna(\"\").str.lower()\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Health-need vocabulary size:\", len(need_vectorizer.vocabulary_))\n",
        "print(\"Service vocabulary size:\", len(service_vectorizer.vocabulary_))\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "V6tMAg7m_HcD"
      },
      "source": [
        "**ML-based health need matcher**\n",
        "\n",
        "Mirrors `match_health_needs()`, but computes `Health_Need_Score` and `Service_Score` from cosine similarity instead of `fuzz.token_set_ratio`. It plugs into the same `apply_clinical_scores`, `calculate_final_score`, and `apply_emergency_boost` functions defined earlier, so only the two similarity signals change."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "ff54a484"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def match_health_needs_ml(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy):\n",
        "    \"\"\"ML equivalent of match_health_needs(): TF-IDF + cosine similarity\n",
        "    instead of character-level fuzzy matching.\"\"\"\n",
        "    results = taxonomy_df.copy()\n",
        "    query_clean = preprocess_text(query)\n",
        "    need_vec = need_vectorizer.transform([query_clean])\n",
        "    results[\"Health_Need_Score\"] = (\n",
        "        cosine_similarity(need_vec, need_tfidf_matrix).flatten() * 100\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    service_vec = service_vectorizer.transform([query_clean])\n",
        "    results[\"Service_Score\"] = (\n",
        "        cosine_similarity(service_vec, service_tfidf_matrix).flatten() * 100\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return results\n",
        "\n",
        "def recommend_health_needs_ml(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy, top_n=5):\n",
        "    \"\"\"ML equivalent of recommend_health_needs(): same clinical-signal boost\n",
        "    and emergency boost, but query <-> health need similarity is computed\n",
        "    with TF-IDF cosine similarity instead of fuzzy string matching.\"\"\"\n",
        "    results = match_health_needs_ml(query, taxonomy_df)\n",
        "    results = apply_clinical_scores(results, query)  # unchanged: rule-based safety net\n",
        "    results = calculate_final_score(results)  # unchanged: same weighted combination\n",
        "    results = apply_emergency_boost(results)  # unchanged: same emergency boost\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\"Final_Match_Score\", ascending=False)\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)\n"
      ]
    },

        "comparison_rows = []\n",
        "for query in test_queries:\n",
        "    fuzzy_top = recommend_health_needs(query, top_n=1).iloc[0]\n",
        "    ml_top = recommend_health_needs_ml(query, top_n=1).iloc[0]\n",
        "    comparison_rows.append(\n",
        "        {\n",
        "            \"Query\": query,\n",
        "            \"Fuzzy match (rapidfuzz)\": fuzzy_top[\"User_Health_Need\"],\n",
        "            \"Fuzzy score\": round(fuzzy_top[\"Final_Match_Score\"], 1),\n",
        "            \"ML match (TF-IDF)\": ml_top[\"User_Health_Need\"],\n",
        "            \"ML score\": round(ml_top[\"Final_Match_Score\"], 1),\n",
        "            \"Agree?\": (\n",
        "                \"Yes\"\n",
        "                if fuzzy_top[\"User_Health_Need\"] == ml_top[\"User_Health_Need\"]\n",
        "                else \"No\"\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "comparison_df = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)\n",
        "display(comparison_df)\n"

        "def recommend_health_needs_embedding(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy, top_n=5):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Preprocess query\n",
        "    query = preprocess_text(query)\n",
        "    # Convert query into embedding\n",
        "    query_embedding = embedding_model.encode([query])\n",
        "    # Calculate similarity with taxonomy\n",
        "    similarities = cosine_similarity(query_embedding, taxonomy_embeddings)[0]\n",
        "    # Add similarity score\n",
        "    results = taxonomy_df.copy()\n",
        "    results[\"Embedding_Score\"] = similarities * 100\n",
        "    # Rank results\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\"Embedding_Score\", ascending=False)\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)\n"

              "        const element = document.querySelector('#df-1f1b2f15-04ec-46db-a63f-aa5cff783c15');\n",
              "        const dataTable =\n",
              "          await google.colab.kernel.invokeFunction('convertToInteractive',\n",
              "                                                    [key], {});\n",
              "        if (!dataTable) return;\n",
              "\n",
              "        const docLinkHtml = 'Like what you see? Visit the ' +\n",
              "          '<a target=\"_blank\" href=https://colab.research.google.com/notebooks/data_table.ipynb>data table notebook</a>'\n",
              "          + ' to learn more about interactive tables.';\n",
              "        element.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "        dataTable['output_type'] = 'display_data';\n",
              "        await google.colab.output.renderOutput(dataTable, element);\n",
              "        const docLink = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "        docLink.innerHTML = docLinkHtml;\n",
              "        element.appendChild(docLink);\n",
              "      }\n",

      "source": [
        "# Embedding-based health need recommendation\n",
        "\n",
        "# Install if not already installed\n",
        "!pip install sentence-transformers -q\n",
        "\n",
        "from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "# Load embedding model\n",
        "\n",
        "embedding_model = SentenceTransformer(\"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create taxonomy embeddings\n",
        "\n",
        "taxonomy_embeddings = embedding_model.encode(\n",
        "    taxonomy[\"Search_Index\"].tolist(),\n",
        "    show_progress_bar=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Embedding-based recommendation function\n",
        "\n",
        "def recommend_health_needs_embedding(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy, top_n=5):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Standardize query\n",
        "    query = preprocess_text(query)\n",
        "    # Convert query into embedding\n",
        "    query_embedding = embedding_model.encode([query])\n",
        "    # Calculate similarity with taxonomy\n",
        "    similarity_scores = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "        query_embedding,\n",
        "        taxonomy_embeddings\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "    # Create results table\n",
        "    results = taxonomy_df.copy()\n",
        "    results[\"Embedding_Score\"] = similarity_scores * 100\n",
        "    # Rank by similarity\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\n",
        "        \"Embedding_Score\",\n",
        "        ascending=False\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Compare fuzzy matching vs embedding matching\n",
        "\n",
        "comparison_rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for query in test_queries:\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Existing fuzzy recommendation\n",
        "    fuzzy_top = recommend_health_needs(\n",
        "        query,\n",
        "        top_n=1\n",
        "    ).iloc[0]\n",
        "    # New embedding recommendation\n",
        "    embedding_top = recommend_health_needs_embedding(\n",
        "        query,\n",
        "        top_n=1\n",
        "    ).iloc[0]\n",
        "    comparison_rows.append({\n",
        "        \"Query\": query,\n",
        "        \"Fuzzy Match\": fuzzy_top[\"User_Health_Need\"],\n",
        "        \"Fuzzy Score\": round(\n",
        "            fuzzy_top[\"Final_Match_Score\"],\n",
        "            1\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"Embedding Match\": embedding_top[\"User_Health_Need\"],\n",
        "        \"Embedding Score\": round(\n",
        "            embedding_top[\"Embedding_Score\"],\n",
        "            1\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        \"Agree?\":\n",
        "        \"Yes\"\n",
        "        if fuzzy_top[\"User_Health_Need\"] ==\n",
        "           embedding_top[\"User_Health_Need\"]\n",
        "        else \"No\"\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "comparison_df = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "display(comparison_df)"
      ]
    },

        "# Embeddings\n",
        "\n",
        "embedding_model = SentenceTransformer(\"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\")\n",
        "\n",
        "taxonomy_embeddings = embedding_model.encode(\n",
        "    taxonomy[\"Expanded_Search_Index\"].tolist(), show_progress_bar=True\n",
        ")\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "GFBgRbbqHr_M"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "# Hybrid query matching\n",
        "\n",
        "def recommend_health_needs_hybrid(query, taxonomy_df=taxonomy, top_n=5):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Expand abbreviations and normalize text\n",
        "    query_processed = expand_synonyms(query)\n",
        "    # Embedding similarity\n",
        "    query_embedding = embedding_model.encode([query_processed])\n",
        "    embedding_scores = cosine_similarity(query_embedding, taxonomy_embeddings)[0]\n",
        "    # Word TF-IDF similarity\n",
        "    query_word = word_vectorizer.transform([query_processed])\n",
        "    word_scores = cosine_similarity(query_word, word_matrix)[0]\n",
        "    # Character TF-IDF similarity\n",
        "    query_char = char_vectorizer.transform([query_processed])\n",
        "    char_scores = cosine_similarity(query_char, char_matrix)[0]\n",
        "    # Combine scores\n",
        "    final_scores = embedding_scores * 0.45 + word_scores * 0.25 + char_scores * 0.15\n",
        "    results = taxonomy_df.copy()\n",
        "    results[\"Embedding_Score\"] = embedding_scores * 100\n",
        "    results[\"Word_TFIDF_Score\"] = word_scores * 100\n",
        "    results[\"Character_TFIDF_Score\"] = char_scores * 100\n",
        "    results[\"Hybrid_Score\"] = final_scores * 100\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\"Hybrid_Score\", ascending=False)\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(drop=True)\n"
      ]
    },

      "metadata": {
        "id": "NE_YBrymQ69Q"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "# Candidate retrieval\n",
        "\n",
        "from rapidfuzz import fuzz\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "SERVICE_MATCH_THRESHOLD = 70\n",
        "\n",
        "# Calculate best service similarity score\n",
        "\n",
        "def best_service_match_score(services_list, target_service):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Finds the highest similarity between the required service\n",
        "    and any service offered by a facility.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    if not isinstance(services_list, list) or len(services_list) == 0:\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "    target_service = str(target_service).lower()\n",
        "    scores = [\n",
        "        fuzz.token_set_ratio(target_service, str(service).lower())\n",
        "        for service in services_list\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    return max(scores)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Retrieve candidate facilities\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_candidate_facilities(\n",
        "    top_need, master_df=master_facilities, threshold=SERVICE_MATCH_THRESHOLD\n",
        "):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Retrieves facilities that can provide the identified health need.\n",
        "    Inputs:\n",
        "    -------\n",
        "    top_need:\n",
        "        The matched taxonomy row from the hybrid query model.\n",
        "    master_df:\n",
        "        Master facility dataset.\n",
        "    Retrieval logic:\n",
        "    ----------------\n",
        "    1. Extract required service from taxonomy.\n",
        "    2. Extract minimum KEPH level requirement from taxonomy.\n",
        "    3. Remove facilities below required KEPH level.\n",
        "    4. Remove inactive facilities.\n",
        "    5. Match facility services using fuzzy matching.\n",
        "    6. Fall back to service group if no direct matches exist.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    candidates = master_df.copy()\n",
        "    # 1. Extract requirements from taxonomy output\n",
        "    required_service = top_need[\"Standardized_Services\"]\n",
        "    service_group = top_need[\"Service_Group\"]\n",
        "    minimum_level = top_need[\"Minimum_KEPH_Level_Numeric\"]\n",
        "    # 2. Filter facilities by KEPH capability\n",
        "    if pd.notna(minimum_level):\n",
        "        candidates = candidates[candidates[\"Keph_Level_Numeric\"] >= minimum_level]\n",
        "    # 3. Keep operational facilities only\n",
        "    if \"Operational_Status\" in candidates.columns:\n",
        "        candidates = candidates[\n",
        "            candidates[\"Operational_Status\"]\n",
        "            .astype(str)\n",
        "            .str.lower()\n",
        "            .isin([\"yes\", \"active\", \"operational\", \"true\"])\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    # 4. Match required standardized service\n",
        "    candidates[\"Service_Match_Score\"] = candidates[\"Services_Parsed\"].apply(\n",
        "        lambda services: best_service_match_score(services, required_service)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    matched = candidates[candidates[\"Service_Match_Score\"] >= threshold]\n",
        "    # 5. Fallback to service group\n",
        "    if matched.empty:\n",
        "        candidates[\"Service_Match_Score\"] = candidates[\"Services_Parsed\"].apply(\n",
        "            lambda services: best_service_match_score(services, service_group)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        matched = candidates[candidates[\"Service_Match_Score\"] >= threshold]\n",
        "    return matched.reset_index(drop=True)\n"
      ]
    },

      "metadata": {
        "id": "qeI_LdDmTjAU"
      },
      "source": [
        "# Feature engineering\n",
        "\n",
        "Each candidate facility is scored on three features:\n",
        "\n",
        "- Service match score — computed during candidate retrieval.\n",
        "- Facility level score — rewards meeting or comfortably exceeding the required KEPH level, without over-favoring facilities that are far more specialized than needed.\n",
        "- Distance score — haversine distance between user and facility, converted to a 0-100 score.\n",
        "\n",
        "If location or coordinate columns aren't available, distance is left out (candidates get a neutral score) instead of failing the pipeline."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "qlZqW7BXSBgg"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def haversine_distance(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Great-circle distance between two lat/lon points, in kilometers.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    R = 6371.0  # Earth radius in km\n",
        "    lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2 = map(radians, [lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2])\n",
        "    dlat = lat2 - lat1\n",
        "    dlon = lon2 - lon1\n",
        "    a = sin(dlat / 2) ** 2 + cos(lat1) * cos(lat2) * sin(dlon / 2) ** 2\n",
        "    c = 2 * atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(1 - a))\n",
        "    return R * c\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "TimofO9FT8J_"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "2ee32557"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def engineer_facility_features(\n",
        "    candidates, min_keph_level, user_lat=None, user_lon=None, lat_col=None, lon_col=None\n",
        "):\n",
        "\n",
        "    candidates = candidates.copy()\n",
        "    lat_col = lat_col or \"Latitude\"\n",
        "    lon_col = lon_col or \"Longitude\"\n",
        "    # Facility level: meeting the minimum is rewarded; each level above the minimum\n",
        "    # trims the score slightly, capped so it never drops below 40, to avoid always\n",
        "    # pushing users toward the largest hospital when a smaller facility is adequate\n",
        "    if min_keph_level is not None:\n",
        "        level_gap = candidates[\"Keph_Level_Numeric\"] - min_keph_level\n",
        "        candidates[\"Facility_Level_Score\"] = np.where(\n",
        "            level_gap < 0, 0, 100 - np.clip(level_gap * 10, 0, 60)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        candidates[\"Facility_Level_Score\"] = 50  # neutral if minimum level is unknown\n",
        "    # Distance: closer facilities score higher, relative to the furthest candidate returned\n",
        "    have_location = (\n",
        "        user_lat is not None\n",
        "        and user_lon is not None\n",
        "        and lat_col is not None\n",
        "        and lon_col is not None\n",
        "        and lat_col in candidates.columns\n",
        "        and lon_col in candidates.columns\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if have_location:\n",
        "        candidates = candidates.dropna(subset=[lat_col, lon_col])\n",
        "        candidates[\"Distance_Km\"] = candidates.apply(\n",
        "            lambda r: haversine_distance(user_lat, user_lon, r[lat_col], r[lon_col]),\n",
        "            axis=1,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        max_distance = candidates[\"Distance_Km\"].max() or 1\n",
        "        candidates[\"Distance_Score\"] = 100 * (\n",
        "            1 - candidates[\"Distance_Km\"] / (max_distance + 1e-9)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        candidates[\"Distance_Km\"] = np.nan\n",
        "        candidates[\"Distance_Score\"] = 50  # neutral score when location is unavailable\n",
        "    return candidates\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "wFyHwPqSUbhd"
      },
      "source": [
        "# Weighted scoring model\n",
        "\n",
        "The three features combine into one Facility Score with fixed weights. Service match carries the most weight, since a facility without the required service isn't a valid recommendation. Distance is weighted above facility level, since most routine needs are served fine once the minimum KEPH level is met, making proximity the deciding factor.\n",
        "\n",
        "Weights live in a dictionary so they can be tuned later if feedback data becomes available."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "1f7576d8"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "FACILITY_SCORE_WEIGHTS = {\"service\": 0.50, \"level\": 0.20, \"distance\": 0.30}\n",
        "\n",
        "def score_facilities(candidates):\n",
        "\n",
        "    candidates = candidates.copy()\n",
        "    candidates[\"Facility_Score\"] = (\n",
        "        candidates[\"Service_Match_Score\"] * FACILITY_SCORE_WEIGHTS[\"service\"]\n",
        "        + candidates[\"Facility_Level_Score\"] * FACILITY_SCORE_WEIGHTS[\"level\"]\n",
        "        + candidates[\"Distance_Score\"] * FACILITY_SCORE_WEIGHTS[\"distance\"]\n",
        "    ).round(2)\n",
        "    return candidates.sort_values(\"Facility_Score\", ascending=False).reset_index(\n",
        "        drop=True\n",
        "    )\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "metadata": {
        "id": "HXu0h0K5Vhka"
      },
      "source": [
        "# Explainable recommendations\n",
        "\n",
        "Generates a short explanation for each recommended facility — service offered, distance where available, and facility level — so the user understands why it was suggested."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "7e0f78aa"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def explain_recommendation(row, standardized_service):\n",
        "\n",
        "    parts = [f\"Offers {standardized_service}.\"]\n",
        "    if pd.notna(row.get(\"Distance_Km\", np.nan)):\n",
        "        parts.append(f\"Located {row['Distance_Km']:.1f} km away.\")\n",
        "    if pd.notna(row.get(\"Keph_Level_Numeric\", np.nan)):\n",
        "        parts.append(\n",
        "            f\"KEPH Level {int(row['Keph_Level_Numeric'])} {row['Facility Type']}.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        parts.append(f\"{row['Facility Type']}.\")\n",
        "    return \" \".join(parts)\n"
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "b7874918"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "def describe_distance(distance_km):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Turns a raw distance in km into an everyday-language phrase.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if pd.isna(distance_km):\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    if distance_km < 1:\n",
        "        return f\"just {distance_km * 1000:.0f} m away, practically next door\"\n",
        "    if distance_km < 3:\n",
        "        return f\"about {distance_km:.1f} km away, a short trip\"\n",
        "    if distance_km < 10:\n",
        "        return f\"about {distance_km:.1f} km away\"\n",
        "    return f\"about {distance_km:.1f} km away, a bit of a distance if closer options don't work out\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def describe_facility_level(row):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Turns raw KEPH level + facility type columns into a plain-language phrase.\"\"\"\n",
        "    ftype = str(row.get(\"Facility Type\", \"facility\")).rstrip(\"s\").lower()\n",
        "    level = row.get(\"Keph_Level_Numeric\", np.nan)\n",
        "    level_score = row.get(\"Facility_Level_Score\", 100)\n",
        "    if pd.isna(level):\n",
        "        return f\"a {ftype}\"\n",
        "    qualifier = \"well-equipped\" if level_score >= 90 else \"capable\"\n",
        "    return f\"a {qualifier} Level {int(level)} {ftype}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "def explain_recommendation_friendly(row, standardized_service):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Plain-language, user-friendly version of explain_recommendation().\"\"\"\n",
        "    sentence = f\"This is {describe_facility_level(row)} offering {standardized_service}\"\n",
        "    distance_phrase = describe_distance(row.get(\"Distance_Km\", np.nan))\n",
        "    if distance_phrase:\n",
        "        sentence += f\", and it's {distance_phrase}.\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        sentence += \".\"\n",
        "    return sentence\n",
        "\n",
        "CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD_LOW = 45\n",
        "CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD_HIGH = 80\n",
        "\n",
        "def confidence_intro(top_need):\n",
        "    \"\"\"A short, honest intro message reflecting how confident the match is,\n",
        "    plus an urgency notice for emergency-flagged needs. Returns a list of\n",
        "    lines to print before the recommendations.\"\"\"\n",
        "    score = top_need[\"Final_Match_Score\"]\n",
        "    need = top_need[\"User_Health_Need\"]\n",
        "    lines = []\n",
        "    if str(top_need.get(\"Emergency_Related\", \"No\")) == \"Yes\":\n",
        "        lines.append(\n",
        "            \"This sounds like it could be an emergency. If you are in immediate danger, \"\n",
        "            \"please go to the nearest hospital or call emergency services right away. \"\n",
        "            \"Here are the nearest facilities that can help:\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    elif score >= CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD_HIGH:\n",
        "        lines.append(f\"Here's what I found for {need.lower()}:\")\n",
        "    elif score >= CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD_LOW:\n",
        "        lines.append(\n",
        "            f\"I think you're asking about {need.lower()} — here's what I found. \"\n",
        "            \"If that's not quite right, try adding a bit more detail to your question.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        lines.append(\n",
        "            f\"I'm not fully sure I understood — the closest match I found was \"\n",
        "            f\"'{need}', but I'm not confident about it. Could you describe what \"\n",
        "            \"you're experiencing or looking for in a little more detail?\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    return lines\n"
      ]
    },

        "# Auto-detect location / status columns\n",
        "\n",
        "def find_column(df, keywords, exclude=None):\n",
        "    exclude = exclude or []\n",
        "    for col in df.columns:\n",
        "        cl = col.lower()\n",
        "        if any(ex in cl for ex in exclude):\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        if any(k in cl for k in keywords):\n",
        "            return col\n",
        "    return None\n",
        "\n",
        "LAT_COL = find_column(master_facilities, [\"latitude\", \"lat_\"]) or find_column(\n",
        "    master_facilities, [\"lat\"]\n",
        ")\n",
        "LON_COL = find_column(master_facilities, [\"longitude\", \"long\"]) or find_column(\n",
        "    master_facilities, [\"lon\"]\n",
        ")\n",
        "COUNTY_COL = find_column(master_facilities, [\"county\"])\n",
        "STATUS_COL = find_column(master_facilities, [\"published\"]) or find_column(\n",
        "    master_facilities,\n",
        "    [\"operation status\", \"operational status\", \"facility status\", \"status\"],\n",
        "    exclude=[\"khis\", \"regulat\"],\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"All columns in Master Facility List:\")\n",
        "print(list(master_facilities.columns))\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"Detected Latitude column: \", LAT_COL)\n",
        "print(\"Detected Longitude column:\", LON_COL)\n",
        "print(\"Detected County column:   \", COUNTY_COL)\n",
        "print(\"Detected Status column:   \", STATUS_COL)\n",
        "print()\n",
        "print(\"If any of these are wrong or None, set them manually here, e.g.:\")\n",
        "print('  LAT_COL = \"Latitude\"')\n",
        "print('  LON_COL = \"Longitude\"')\n",
        "\n",
        "# ---- Manual overrides (uncomment and edit if auto-detection above is wrong) ----\n",
        "# LAT_COL = \"Latitude\"\n",
        "# LON_COL = \"Longitude\"\n",
        "# COUNTY_COL = \"County\"\n",
        "# STATUS_COL = \"Published\"  # or \"Facility Status\" if your data uses that name\n"
      ]

      "source": [
        "**End-to-end recommendation pipeline**\n",
        "\n",
        "Ties everything together: match query to health need, retrieve candidates, engineer features, score, attach explanations. This is the entry point the rest of the system would call."
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "BAkgXGUgnggA"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "# Full recommendation pipeline\n",
        "\n",
        "def recommend_facilities(\n",
        "    user_query,\n",
        "    taxonomy_df=taxonomy,\n",
        "    master_df=master_facilities,\n",
        "    county=\"All Counties\",\n",
        "    user_lat=None,\n",
        "    user_lon=None,\n",
        "    top_n=5,\n",
        "    lat_col=None,\n",
        "    lon_col=None,\n",
        "    status_col=None,\n",
        "    method=\"hybrid\",\n",
        "    verbose=True,\n",
        "):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # 1. select matching method\n",
        "    match_functions = {\n",
        "        \"hybrid\": recommend_health_needs_hybrid,\n",
        "        \"ml\": recommend_health_needs_ml,\n",
        "        \"fuzzy\": recommend_health_needs,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    match_fn = match_functions.get(method, recommend_health_needs_hybrid)\n",
        "    if verbose:\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "        print(\"QUERY UNDERSTANDING\")\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "        print(\"Matching Method:\", method)\n",
        "        print(\"Function Used:\", match_fn.__name__)\n",
        "    # 2. identify health need\n",
        "    matched_needs = match_fn(user_query, taxonomy_df, top_n=1)\n",
        "    top_need = matched_needs.iloc[0]\n",
        "    if verbose:\n",
        "        print(\"\\nMatched Health Need:\")\n",
        "        print(top_need[\"User_Health_Need\"])\n",
        "        print(\"\\nStandardized Service:\")\n",
        "        print(top_need[\"Standardized_Services\"])\n",
        "        print(\"\\nService Group:\")\n",
        "        print(top_need[\"Service_Group\"])\n",
        "        print(\"\\nMinimum KEPH Level:\")\n",
        "        print(top_need[\"Minimum_KEPH_Level_Numeric\"])\n",
        "    # 3. retrieve candidates\n",
        "    candidates = get_candidate_facilities(\n",
        "        top_need, master_df, threshold=SERVICE_MATCH_THRESHOLD\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    # County filter\n",
        "    if county != \"All Counties\" and \"County\" in candidates.columns:\n",
        "        candidates = candidates[\n",
        "            candidates[\"County\"].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\n",
        "            == county.strip().lower()\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    if verbose:\n",
        "        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 40)\n",
        "        print(\"CANDIDATE RETRIEVAL\")\n",
        "        print(\"=\" * 40)\n",
        "        print(\"County:\", county)\n",
        "        print(\"Candidates:\", len(candidates))\n",
        "    display_cols = [\n",
        "        # Facility details\n",
        "        \"Facility Name\",\n",
        "        \"County\",\n",
        "        \"Facility Type\",\n",
        "        # Coordinates for map\n",
        "        \"Latitude\",\n",
        "        \"Longitude\",\n",
        "        # Facility capability badges\n",
        "        \"Open 24 hours\",\n",
        "        \"Open weekends\",\n",
        "        \"NHIF accreditation\",\n",
        "        \"Owner\",\n",
        "        # Ranking information\n",
        "        \"Keph_Level_Numeric\",\n",
        "        \"Distance_Km\",\n",
        "        \"Service_Match_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Facility_Level_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Distance_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Facility_Score\",\n",
        "        # Explanation\n",
        "        \"Explanation\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    if candidates.empty:\n",
        "        if verbose:\n",
        "            print(\"\\nNo facilities found.\")\n",
        "        return (top_need, pd.DataFrame(columns=display_cols))\n",
        "    # 4. feature engineering\n",
        "    candidates = engineer_facility_features(\n",
        "        candidates,\n",
        "        top_need[\"Minimum_KEPH_Level_Numeric\"],\n",
        "        user_lat,\n",
        "        user_lon,\n",
        "        lat_col=lat_col,\n",
        "        lon_col=lon_col,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    # 5. facility scoring\n",
        "    ranked = score_facilities(candidates)\n",
        "    # 6. explanations\n",
        "    ranked[\"Explanation\"] = ranked.apply(\n",
        "        lambda row: explain_recommendation_friendly(\n",
        "            row, top_need[\"Standardized_Services\"]\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        axis=1,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    # 7. standardize confidence\n",
        "    if method == \"hybrid\":\n",
        "        confidence = top_need.get(\"Hybrid_Score\", top_need.get(\"Final_Match_Score\", 0))\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        confidence = top_need.get(\"Final_Match_Score\", 0)\n",
        "    top_need[\"Recommendation_Confidence\"] = round(float(confidence), 1)\n",
        "    # 8. clean output\n",
        "    numeric_columns = [\n",
        "        \"Distance_Km\",\n",
        "        \"Service_Match_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Facility_Level_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Distance_Score\",\n",
        "        \"Facility_Score\",\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    for column in numeric_columns:\n",
        "        if column in ranked.columns:\n",
        "            ranked[column] = ranked[column].round(1)\n",
        "    if \"Distance_Km\" in ranked.columns:\n",
        "        ranked[\"Distance_Km\"] = ranked[\"Distance_Km\"].fillna(\"N/A\")\n",
        "    # 9. return results\n",
        "    print(ranked.columns.tolist())\n",
        "    return (top_need, ranked[display_cols].head(top_n))\n"
      ]
    },

              "          '<a target=\"_blank\" href=https://colab.research.google.com/notebooks/data_table.ipynb>data table notebook</a>'\n",
              "          + ' to learn more about interactive tables.';\n",
              "        element.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "        dataTable['output_type'] = 'display_data';\n",
              "        await google.colab.output.renderOutput(dataTable, element);\n",
              "        const docLink = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "        docLink.innerHTML = docLinkHtml;\n",
              "        element.appendChild(docLink);\n",
              "      }\n",

        "import folium\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_facility_map(recommendations, user_lat=None, user_lon=None):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Empty map\n",
        "    if recommendations.empty:\n",
        "        return folium.Map(location=[0, 37], zoom_start=6)\n",
        "    # Check coordinates exist\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        \"Latitude\" not in recommendations.columns\n",
        "        or \"Longitude\" not in recommendations.columns\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        return folium.Map(location=[0, 37], zoom_start=6)\n",
        "    # Remove missing coordinates\n",
        "    map_data = recommendations.dropna(subset=[\"Latitude\", \"Longitude\"])\n",
        "    if map_data.empty:\n",
        "        return folium.Map(location=[0, 37], zoom_start=6)\n",
        "    # Map center\n",
        "    if user_lat is not None and user_lon is not None:\n",
        "        center = [user_lat, user_lon]\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        center = [map_data[\"Latitude\"].mean(), map_data[\"Longitude\"].mean()]\n",
        "    m = folium.Map(location=center, zoom_start=12)\n",
        "    # User location\n",
        "    if user_lat is not None and user_lon is not None:\n",
        "        folium.Marker(\n",
        "            [user_lat, user_lon],\n",
        "            popup=\"📍 Your Location\",\n",
        "            icon=folium.Icon(color=\"blue\"),\n",
        "        ).add_to(m)\n",
        "    # Facility markers\n",
        "    for _, row in map_data.iterrows():\n",
        "        popup = f\"\"\"\n",
        "        <b>{row['Facility Name']}</b><br>\n",
        "        County: {row.get('County','')}<br>\n",
        "        Distance: {row.get('Distance_Km','N/A')} km<br>\n",
        "        Score: {row.get('Facility_Score','N/A')}<br>\n",
        "        <br>\n",
        "        {row.get('Explanation','')}\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        folium.Marker(\n",
        "            [row[\"Latitude\"], row[\"Longitude\"]],\n",
        "            popup=popup,\n",
        "            icon=folium.Icon(color=\"orange\"),\n",
        "        ).add_to(m)\n",
        "    return m\n"
      ]
    },

      "metadata": {
        "id": "vcxZh1-FNsRJ"

      "source": [
        "# Create facility recommendation cards\n",
        "\n",
        "def create_facility_cards(recommendations):\n",
        "\n",
        "    if recommendations.empty:\n",
        "        return \"\"\"\n",
        "# 🏥 Recommended Facilities\n",
        "\n",
        "No facilities found matching your health need.\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    cards = \"\"\"\n",
        "# 🏥 Recommended Facilities\n",
        "\n",
        "Facilities are ranked based on:\n",
        "- Service availability\n",
        "- Facility capability\n",
        "- Location suitability\n",
        "- Recommendation score\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Helper function\n",
        "    def clean_value(value):\n",
        "        if value is None or str(value).strip().lower() in [\"\", \"nan\", \"none\"]:\n",
        "            return \"Unknown\"\n",
        "        return str(value)\n",
        "    # Generate cards\n",
        "    for rank, (_, row) in enumerate(recommendations.iterrows(), start=1):\n",
        "        # Facility information\n",
        "        facility_name = clean_value(row.get(\"Facility Name\"))\n",
        "        county = clean_value(row.get(\"County\"))\n",
        "        facility_type = clean_value(row.get(\"Facility Type\"))\n",
        "        level = clean_value(row.get(\"Keph_Level_Numeric\"))\n",
        "        distance = clean_value(row.get(\"Distance_Km\"))\n",
        "        score = clean_value(row.get(\"Facility_Score\"))\n",
        "        # Facility profile\n",
        "        open_24 = clean_value(row.get(\"Open 24 hours\"))\n",
        "        open_weekends = clean_value(row.get(\"Open weekends\"))\n",
        "        nhif = clean_value(row.get(\"NHIF accreditation\"))\n",
        "        owner = clean_value(row.get(\"Owner\"))\n",
        "        facility_profile = f\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "## 🏷 Facility Profile\n",
        "\n",
        "| Feature | Status |\n",
        "|---|---|\n",
        "| ⏰ Open 24 Hours | {open_24} |\n",
        "| 📅 Open Weekends | {open_weekends} |\n",
        "| 🏥 NHIF Accreditation | {nhif} |\n",
        "| 🏛 Ownership | {owner} |\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Explanation\n",
        "        explanation = clean_value(row.get(\"Explanation\"))\n",
        "        # Ranking label\n",
        "        if rank == 1:\n",
        "            rank_label = \"🥇 Best Match\"\n",
        "        elif rank <= 3:\n",
        "            rank_label = \"⭐ Strong Match\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            rank_label = \"👍 Suitable Option\"\n",
        "        # Build card\n",
        "        cards += f\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "---\n",
        "\n",
        "# {rank}. 🏥 {facility_name}\n",
        "\n",
        "### {rank_label}\n",
        "\n",
        "📍 **County**\n",
        "\n",
        "{county}\n",
        "\n",
        "🏥 **Facility Type**\n",
        "\n",
        "{facility_type}\n",
        "\n",
        "🏥 **Facility Level**\n",
        "\n",
        "Level {level}\n",
        "\n",
        "📏 **Distance**\n",
        "\n",
        "{distance} km\n",
        "\n",
        "🎯 **Recommendation Score**\n",
        "\n",
        "{score}/100\n",
        "\n",
        "{facility_profile}\n",
        "\n",
        "## ✅ Why this facility?\n",
        "\n",
        "{explanation}\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    return cards\n"
      ]
    },

      "execution_count": null,
      "metadata": {
        "id": "pF75Y1AZC8UR"
      },
      "outputs": [],
      "source": [
        "# Afya search function (top 5 recommendations)\n",
        "\n",
        "def afya_search(query, county, use_gps, latitude, longitude, max_distance):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Connects Gradio interface with recommendation engine.\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        1. Recommendation summary\n",
        "        2. Interactive map\n",
        "        3. Recommendation cards\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    # Empty query\n",
        "    if not query or query.strip() == \"\":\n",
        "        empty_map = create_facility_map(pd.DataFrame(), None, None)\n",
        "        return (\n",
        "            \"\"\"\n",
        "# 🧠 Recommendation Summary\n",
        "\n",
        "Please enter a health concern.\n",
        "\"\"\",\n",
        "            empty_map._repr_html_(),\n",
        "            \"No recommendations available.\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "    # Gps handling\n",
        "    if use_gps and latitude is not None and longitude is not None:\n",
        "        user_lat = float(latitude)\n",
        "        user_lon = float(longitude)\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        user_lat = None\n",
        "        user_lon = None\n",
        "    # Run recommendation engine\n",
        "    top_need, recommendations = recommend_facilities(\n",
        "        user_query=query,\n",
        "        taxonomy_df=taxonomy,\n",
        "        master_df=master_facilities,\n",
        "        county=county,\n",
        "        user_lat=user_lat,\n",
        "        user_lon=user_lon,\n",
        "        top_n=20,\n",
        "        method=\"hybrid\",\n",
        "        verbose=False,\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    # Distance filter\n",
        "    if (\n",
        "        user_lat is not None\n",
        "        and not recommendations.empty\n",
        "        and \"Distance_Km\" in recommendations.columns\n",
        "    ):\n",
        "        recommendations = recommendations.copy()\n",
        "        recommendations[\"Distance_Km\"] = pd.to_numeric(\n",
        "            recommendations[\"Distance_Km\"], errors=\"coerce\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        recommendations = recommendations[\n",
        "            recommendations[\"Distance_Km\"].isna()\n",
        "            | (recommendations[\"Distance_Km\"] <= max_distance)\n",
        "        ]\n",
        "    # Keep only top 5\n",
        "    recommendations = recommendations.head(5)\n",
        "    # Summary\n",
        "    confidence = top_need.get(\"Recommendation_Confidence\", 0)\n",
        "    summary = f\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# 🧠 Recommendation Summary\n",
        "\n",
        "### Health Need\n",
        "\n",
        "**{top_need['User_Health_Need']}**\n",
        "\n",
        "### Recommended Service\n",
        "\n",
        "**{top_need['Standardized_Services']}**\n",
        "\n",
        "### Recommendation Confidence\n",
        "\n",
        "**{confidence:.1f}%**\n",
        "\n",
        "Showing the top 5 suitable facilities based on:\n",
        "- Service availability\n",
        "- Facility capability\n",
        "- Distance\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Create map\n",
        "    facility_map = create_facility_map(recommendations, user_lat, user_lon)\n",
        "    # Create cards\n",
        "    facility_cards = create_facility_cards(recommendations)\n",
        "    # Return\n",
        "    return (summary, facility_map._repr_html_(), facility_cards)\n"
      ]
    },

        {
          "name": "stdout",

        "# Afyaguide final mvp interface\n",
        "\n",
        "import gradio as gr\n",
        "\n",
        "# County list\n",
        "\n",
        "counties = sorted(master_facilities[\"County\"].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())\n",
        "\n",
        "counties.insert(0, \"All Counties\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Orange theme\n",
        "\n",
        "theme = gr.themes.Default(\n",
        "    primary_hue=\"orange\", secondary_hue=\"orange\", neutral_hue=\"slate\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Build app\n",
        "\n",
        "with gr.Blocks(title=\"AfyaGuide\", theme=theme) as demo:\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Header\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# 🏥 AfyaGuide\n",
        "\n",
        "## Intelligent Health Facility Recommendation System\n",
        "\n",
        "Find suitable health facilities based on your health concern,\n",
        "location, and facility capability.\n",
        "\n",
        "---\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Input section\n",
        "    with gr.Group():\n",
        "        gr.Markdown(\"## 🩺 Describe Your Health Concern\")\n",
        "        query_input = gr.Textbox(\n",
        "            lines=4,\n",
        "            placeholder=\"Example: My child has had fever for three days and refuses to eat...\",\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        gr.Examples(\n",
        "            examples=[\n",
        "                [\"My child has had a high fever for three days.\"],\n",
        "                [\"I need family planning services.\"],\n",
        "                [\"I am pregnant and need my first ANC visit.\"],\n",
        "                [\"I need HIV testing near me.\"],\n",
        "                [\"My wife delivered yesterday and is bleeding heavily.\"],\n",
        "            ],\n",
        "            inputs=query_input,\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        with gr.Row():\n",
        "            county_input = gr.Dropdown(\n",
        "                choices=counties, value=\"All Counties\", label=\"County\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "            distance_input = gr.Slider(\n",
        "                minimum=5, maximum=100, value=20, step=5, label=\"Maximum Distance (km)\"\n",
        "            )\n",
        "        use_gps = gr.Checkbox(label=\"📍 Use GPS Coordinates\")\n",
        "        with gr.Row():\n",
        "            latitude_input = gr.Number(label=\"Latitude\", precision=6)\n",
        "            longitude_input = gr.Number(label=\"Longitude\", precision=6)\n",
        "        search_button = gr.Button(\"🔍 Find Health Facilities\", variant=\"primary\")\n",
        "    # Summary\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"---\")\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"## 🧠 Recommendation Summary\")\n",
        "    summary_card = gr.Markdown(\"\"\"\n",
        "Enter a health concern and click search.\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Map\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"---\")\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"## 📍 Facility Location Map\")\n",
        "    facility_map = gr.HTML()\n",
        "    # Results table\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"---\")\n",
        "    gr.Markdown(\"## 🏥 Recommended Facilities\")\n",
        "    recommendations = gr.Markdown(\"\"\"\n",
        "Your recommended facilities will appear here.\n",
        "\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Button connection\n",
        "    search_button.click(\n",

        "            county_input,\n",

        "            longitude_input,\n",
        "            distance_input,\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        outputs=[summary_card, facility_map, recommendations],\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "# Launch\n",
        "\n",
